### [1] NSE:ADANIGREEN — Monthly POC Reversion (LONG)
* **Trigger Details:** Entry: 1493.00 | Stop Loss: 1455.00 | Exit: 1455.00 | Loss: 38.00
* **Mistake Diagnosis:** Broader Index Drag. Trade direction was LONG, but Nifty index closed bearish, dragging this stock down.
* **Auto-Learned Parameter / Constraint Adjustment:** `Index Trend Confluence: Never buy stock CE options when Nifty is trading below its open or has a negative PCR drift (< -0.03).`
